Original range: 96-110
Documentation:

Method code:
96 |   @Override
97 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
98 |     // Don't run externs-checking in sanity check mode. Normalization will
99 |     // remove duplicate VAR declarations, which will make
100 |     // externs look like they have assigns.
101 |     if (!sanityCheck) {
102 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, externs, new NameRefInExternsCheck());
103 |     }
104 | 
105 |     NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(
106 |         compiler, Lists.newArrayList(externs, root), this);
107 |     for (String varName : varsToDeclareInExterns) {
108 |       createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
109 |     }
110 |   }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 250-293
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

   * Construct a new abstract source file.

   *     appear in warning messages emitted by the compiler.

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.Config.buildAnnotationNames(Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/Config.java
Original range: 81-94
Documentation:
/**
   * Create the annotation names from the user-specified
   * annotation whitelist.
   */
Method code:
81 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
82 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {
83 |     ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Annotation> annotationBuilder =
84 |         ImmutableMap.builder();
85 |     annotationBuilder.putAll(Annotation.recognizedAnnotations);
86 |     for (String unrecognizedAnnotation : annotationWhitelist) {
87 |       if (!Annotation.recognizedAnnotations.containsKey(
88 |               unrecognizedAnnotation)) {
89 |         annotationBuilder.put(
90 |             unrecognizedAnnotation, Annotation.NOT_IMPLEMENTED);
91 |       }
92 |     }
93 |     return annotationBuilder.build();
94 |   }

464 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN,
465 |         OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN);
466 | 
467 |     // (string,number,boolean)
468 |     JSType NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN =
469 |         createUnionType(NUMBER_TYPE, STRING_TYPE, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
470 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN,
471 |         NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN);
472 | 
473 |     // (string,number)
474 |     JSType NUMBER_STRING = createUnionType(NUMBER_TYPE, STRING_TYPE);
475 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.NUMBER_STRING, NUMBER_STRING);
476 | 
477 |     // Native object properties are filled in by externs...
478 | 
479 |     // (String, string)
480 |     JSType STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE =
481 |         createUnionType(STRING_OBJECT_TYPE, STRING_TYPE);
482 |     registerNativeType(
483 |         JSTypeNative.STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE, STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE);
484 | 
485 |     // (Number, number)
486 |     JSType NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE =
487 |         createUnionType(NUMBER_OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_TYPE);
488 |     registerNativeType(
489 |         JSTypeNative.NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE);
490 | 
491 |     // unknown function type, i.e. (?...) -> ?
492 |     FunctionType U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE =
493 |         createFunctionType(UNKNOWN_TYPE, true, UNKNOWN_TYPE);
494 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE, U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE);
495 | 
496 |     // unknown constructor type, i.e. (?...) -> ? with the NoObject type
497 |     // as instance type
498 |     FunctionType U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE =
499 |         // This is equivalent to
500 |         // createConstructorType(UNKNOWN_TYPE, true, UNKNOWN_TYPE), but,
501 |         // in addition, overrides getInstanceType() to return the NoObject type
502 |         // instead of a new anonymous object.
503 |         new FunctionType(this, "Function", null,
504 |             createArrowType(
505 |                 createParametersWithVarArgs(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
506 |                 UNKNOWN_TYPE),
507 |             NO_OBJECT_TYPE, null, true, true) {
508 |           private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
509 | 
510 |           @Override public FunctionType getConstructor() {
511 |             return registry.getNativeFunctionType(
512 |                 JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
513 |           }
514 |         };
515 | 
516 |     // The U2U_CONSTRUCTOR is weird, because it's the supertype of its
517 |     // own constructor.
518 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
519 |     registerNativeType(
520 |         JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
521 | 
522 |     FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE.setInstanceType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
523 |     U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE.setImplicitPrototype(FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE);
524 | 
525 |     // least function type, i.e. (All...) -> NoType
526 |     FunctionType LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
527 |         createFunctionType(NO_TYPE, true, ALL_TYPE);
528 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE, LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
529 | 
530 |     // the 'this' object in the global scope
531 |     ObjectType GLOBAL_THIS = createObjectType("global this", null,
532 |         UNKNOWN_TYPE /* to be resolved later */);
533 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS, GLOBAL_THIS);
534 | 
535 |     // greatest function type, i.e. (NoType...) -> All
536 |     FunctionType GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
537 |       createFunctionType(ALL_TYPE, true, NO_TYPE);
538 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE,
539 |         GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
540 | 
541 |     // Register the prototype property. See the comments below in
542 |     // registerPropertyOnType about the bootstrapping process.
543 |     registerPropertyOnType("prototype", OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE);
544 |   }

555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;
559 |     }
560 |

562 |     while (it.hasNext()) {

567 |

569 |   }

     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1865 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1866 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1867 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1868 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1869 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1870 |       if (result.last != null) {
1871 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1872 |       }
1873 |       if (result.first == null) {
1874 |         result.first = n2clone;
1875 |       }
1876 |       result.last = n2clone;
1877 |     }
1878 |     return result;
1879 |   }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 112-199
Documentation:

Method code:
112 |   @Override
113 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
114 |     if (n.getType() != Token.NAME) {
115 |       return;
116 |     }
117 | 
118 |     String varName = n.getString();
119 | 
120 |     // Only a function can have an empty name.
121 |     if (varName.isEmpty()) {
122 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isFunction(parent));
123 | 
124 |       // A function declaration with an empty name passes Rhino,
125 |       // but is supposed to be a syntax error according to the spec.
126 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
127 |         t.report(n, INVALID_FUNCTION_DECL);
128 |       }
129 |       return;
130 |     }
131 | 
132 |     // Check if this is a declaration for a var that has been declared
133 |     // elsewhere. If so, mark it as a duplicate.
134 |     if ((parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
135 |          NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) &&
136 |         varsToDeclareInExterns.contains(varName)) {
137 |       createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
138 | 
139 |       n.addSuppression("duplicate");
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     // Check that the var has been declared.
143 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
144 |     Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(varName);
145 |     if (var == null) {
146 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
147 |         // e.g. [ function foo() {} ], it's okay if "foo" isn't defined in the
148 |         // current scope.
149 |       } else {
150 |         // The extern checks are stricter, don't report a second error.
151 |         if (!strictExternCheck || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
152 |           t.report(n, UNDEFINED_VAR_ERROR, varName);
153 |         }
154 | 
155 |         if (sanityCheck) {
156 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected variable " + varName);
157 |         } else {
158 |           createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
159 |           scope.getGlobalScope().declare(varName, n,
160 |               null, getSynthesizedExternsInput());
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       return;
164 |     }
165 | 
166 |     CompilerInput currInput = t.getInput();
167 |     CompilerInput varInput = var.input;
168 |     if (currInput == varInput || currInput == null || varInput == null) {
169 |       // The variable was defined in the same file. This is fine.
170 |       return;
171 |     }
172 | 
173 |     // Check module dependencies.
174 |     JSModule currModule = currInput.getModule();
175 |     JSModule varModule = varInput.getModule();
176 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
177 |     if (varModule != currModule && varModule != null && currModule != null) {
178 |       if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(currModule, varModule)) {
179 |         // The module dependency was properly declared.
180 |       } else {
181 |         if (!sanityCheck && scope.isGlobal()) {
182 |           if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(varModule, currModule)) {
183 |             // The variable reference violates a declared module dependency.
184 |             t.report(n, VIOLATED_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
185 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
186 |           } else {
187 |             // The variable reference is between two modules that have no
188 |             // dependency relationship. This should probably be considered an
189 |             // error, but just issue a warning for now.
190 |             t.report(n, MISSING_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
191 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
192 |           }
193 |         } else {
194 |           t.report(n, STRICT_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
195 |                    currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
196 |         }
197 |       }
198 |     }
199 |   }

===== M0052 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 222-258
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
222 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
223 |     this.options = options;
224 |     if (errorManager == null) {
225 |       if (outStream == null) {
226 |         setErrorManager(
227 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
228 |       } else {
229 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
230 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
231 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
232 |         setErrorManager(printer);
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
237 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
238 |     guards.add(
239 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
240 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
241 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
242 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
243 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
244 |     }
245 | 
246 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
247 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
248 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
249 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
250 |     // error off.
251 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
252 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
253 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
254 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
255 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
256 |     }
257 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
258 |   }

===== M0053 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getInfoForNameNode(Node)

/**
   * @param nameNode A name node
   * @return The JSDocInfo for the name node

2790 |   static JSDocInfo getInfoForNameNode(Node nameNode) {
2791 |     JSDocInfo info = null;

2802 |     }

2804 |   }

/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2809 |   static JSDocInfo getFunctionInfo(Node n) {
2810 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2811 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2812 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2813 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2814 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2815 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
2816 |         // on ASSIGNs
2817 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2818 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
2819 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2820 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2821 |       }
2822 |     }
2823 |     return fnInfo;
2824 |   }

===== M0059 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 552-570
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
552 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
553 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
554 |         next = c.getNext();
555 |         if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
556 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
557 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
558 |           }
559 | 
560 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
561 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
562 |             c.removeChild(name);
563 |             Node newVar = new Node(
564 |                 Token.VAR, name, n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
565 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
566 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
567 |           }
568 |         }
569 |       }
570 |     }

===== M0060 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 49-86
Documentation:

Method code:
49 |   @Override
50 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
51 |     Node node = error.node;
52 |     if (node != null) {
53 |       for (Node current = node;
54 |            current != null;
55 |            current = current.getParent()) {
56 |         int type = current.getType();
57 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
58 | 
59 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
60 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
61 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
62 |         // a bad idea.
63 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
64 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(current);
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         }
68 | 
69 |         if (info != null) {
70 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
71 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
72 | 
73 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
74 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
75 |             if (guard != null) {
76 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
77 |               if (newLevel != null) {
78 |                 return newLevel;
79 |               }
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 |     return null;
86 |   }

===== M0084 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 422-466
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
424 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
425 |       return true;
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // Union types
429 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
430 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
431 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
432 |       return false;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // record types
436 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
437 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Interfaces
441 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
442 |     // to the interface instance.
443 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
444 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
445 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
446 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
447 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
448 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // other prototype based objects
455 |     if (that != null) {
456 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
457 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
458 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
459 |         // to avoid guessing.
460 |         return true;
461 |       }
462 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return false;
466 |   }

===== M0086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 296-304
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
296 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
297 |       Node ret = transform(n);
298 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
299 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
300 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
301 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
302 |       }
303 |       return ret;
304 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;

785 |       } else {

787 |       }
788 |     }

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 371-386
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
371 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
372 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
373 |       Set<String> directives = null;
374 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
375 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
376 |         if (directives == null) {
377 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
378 |         } else {
379 |           directives.add(directive);
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 | 
383 |       if (directives != null) {
384 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
385 |       }
386 |     }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1052-1189
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1052 |   Node parseInputs() {
1053 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1054 | 
1055 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1056 |     // individual file parse trees.
1057 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1058 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1059 |     }
1060 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1061 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1062 |     }
1063 | 
1064 |     // Parse main js sources.
1065 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1066 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1067 | 
1068 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1069 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1070 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1071 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1072 |     }
1073 | 
1074 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1075 | 
1076 |     try {
1077 |       // Parse externs sources.
1078 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1079 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1080 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1081 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1082 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1083 |           return null;
1084 |         }
1085 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1086 |       }
1087 | 
1088 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1089 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1090 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1091 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1092 | 
1093 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1094 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1095 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1096 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1097 |           }
1098 |         }
1099 | 
1100 |         try {
1101 |           inputs =
1102 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1103 |               .manageDependencies(
1104 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1105 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1106 |           report(JSError.make(
1107 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1108 |           return null;
1109 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1110 |           report(JSError.make(
1111 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1112 |           return null;
1113 |         }
1114 |       }
1115 | 
1116 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1117 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1118 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1119 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1120 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1121 |           return null;
1122 |         }
1123 | 
1124 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1125 |         if (n == null) {
1126 |           continue;
1127 |         }
1128 | 
1129 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1130 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1131 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1132 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1133 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1134 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1135 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1136 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1137 | 
1138 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1139 | 
1140 |             externs.add(input);
1141 |             staleInputs = true;
1142 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1143 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1144 |             staleInputs = true;
1145 |           }
1146 |         }
1147 |       }
1148 | 
1149 |       if (staleInputs) {
1150 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1151 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1152 |       }
1153 | 
1154 |       // Build the AST.
1155 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1156 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1157 |         if (n == null) {
1158 |           continue;
1159 |         }
1160 | 
1161 |         if (devMode) {
1162 |           runSanityCheck();
1163 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1164 |             return null;
1165 |           }
1166 |         }
1167 | 
1168 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1169 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1170 | 
1171 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1172 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1173 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1174 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1175 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1176 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1177 |         }
1178 | 
1179 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1180 |       }
1181 | 
1182 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1183 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1184 | 
1185 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1186 |     } finally {
1187 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1188 |     }
1189 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
93 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
94 |                            String sourceString,
95 |                            Config config,
96 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
97 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
98 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
99 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
100 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
101 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
102 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
103 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
104 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
105 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {

108 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
109 |     }
110 |

112 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;

114 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
115 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
116 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
117 |     } finally {
118 |       Context.exit();
119 |     }

121 |     if (astRoot != null) {
122 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(

124 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);

126 |     return root;

===== M0125 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

===== M0141 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 734-776
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
734 |   @Override
735 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
736 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
737 |       return true;
738 |     }
739 | 
740 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
741 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
742 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
743 |         return true;
744 |       }
745 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
747 |         return false;
748 |       }
749 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
750 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
751 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
752 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
753 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
754 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
755 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
756 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
757 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
758 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
759 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
760 | 
761 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
762 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
763 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
764 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
765 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
766 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
767 | 
768 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
769 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
770 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
771 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
772 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
773 |     }
774 | 
775 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
776 |   }

Method code:
228 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
229 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
230 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
231 |           case Token.VAR:
232 |           case Token.FUNCTION:
233 |           case Token.LP:
234 |             // These are okay.
235 |             break;
236 |           case Token.GETPROP:
237 |             if (n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
238 |               Scope scope = t.getScope();
239 |               Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(n.getString());
240 |               if (var == null) {
241 |                 t.report(n, UNDEFINED_EXTERN_VAR_ERROR, n.getString());
242 |                 varsToDeclareInExterns.add(n.getString());
243 |               }
244 |             }
245 |             break;
246 |           default:
247 |             t.report(n, NAME_REFERENCE_IN_EXTERNS_ERROR, n.getString());
248 | 
249 |             Scope scope = t.getScope();
250 |             Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(n.getString());
251 |             if (var == null) {
252 |               varsToDeclareInExterns.add(n.getString());
253 |             }
254 |             break;
255 |         }

===== M0168 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 120-170
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
121 |       // null is the smallest value
122 |       if (p2 == null) {
123 |         if (p1 == null) {
124 |           return 0;
125 |         } else {
126 |           return P1_GT_P2;
127 |         }
128 |       }
129 | 
130 |       // check level
131 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
132 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
133 |       }
134 | 
135 |       // sourceName comparison
136 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
137 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
138 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
139 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
140 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
141 |           return sourceCompare;
142 |         }
143 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
144 |         return P1_LT_P2;
145 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
146 |         return P1_GT_P2;
147 |       }
148 |       // lineno comparison
149 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
150 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
151 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
152 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
153 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
154 |         return P1_LT_P2;
155 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
156 |         return P1_GT_P2;
157 |       }
158 |       // charno comparison
159 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
160 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
161 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
162 |         return charno1 - charno2;
163 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
164 |         return P1_LT_P2;
165 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
166 |         return P1_GT_P2;
167 |       }
168 |       // description
169 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
170 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array

1172 |       }
1173 |     }
1174 |     return builder.build();

===== M0177 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 895-922
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
895 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
896 |     // unknown
897 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
898 |       return true;
899 |     }
900 |     // equality
901 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
902 |       return true;
903 |     }
904 |     // all type
905 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
906 |       return true;
907 |     }
908 |     // unions
909 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
910 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
911 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
912 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
913 |           return true;
914 |         }
915 |       }
916 |     }
917 |     // named types
918 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
919 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
920 |     }
921 |     return false;
922 |   }

===== M0180 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1404-1427
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1404 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1405 |     boolean eq = false;
1406 | 
1407 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1408 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1409 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1410 |     }
1411 | 
1412 |     if (!eq) {
1413 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1414 |     }
1415 | 
1416 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1417 |     Node n, n2;
1418 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1419 |          res == null && n != null;
1420 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1421 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1422 |       if (res != null) {
1423 |         return res;
1424 |       }
1425 |     }
1426 |     return res;
1427 |   }

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 64-96
Documentation:

Method code:
64 |   @Override
65 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
66 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
67 |     Renamer renamer;
68 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
69 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
70 |       // be a function.
71 |       Preconditions.checkState(
72 |           declarationRoot.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
73 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
74 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
75 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
76 |     } else {
77 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     if (declarationRoot.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
81 |       // Add the function parameters
82 |       Node fnParams = declarationRoot.getFirstChild().getNext();
83 |       for (Node c = fnParams.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
84 |         String name = c.getString();
85 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
86 |       }
87 | 
88 |       // Add the function body declarations
89 |       Node functionBody = declarationRoot.getLastChild();
90 |       findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
91 |     } else {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),

91 |           logger_);

101 |     }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.

106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;

===== M0204 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1908-1910
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1908 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1909 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1910 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 360-368

/**
   * Verifies that at least one module has been provided and that the first one
   * has at least one source code input.
   */
Method code:
360 |   private void checkFirstModule(List<JSModule> modules) {
361 |     if (modules.isEmpty()) {
362 |       report(JSError.make(EMPTY_MODULE_LIST_ERROR));
363 |     } else if (modules.get(0).getInputs().isEmpty() && modules.size() > 1) {
364 |       // The root module may only be empty if there is exactly 1 module.
365 |       report(JSError.make(EMPTY_ROOT_MODULE_ERROR,
366 |           modules.get(0).getName()));
367 |     }
368 |   }

Method code:
57 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
58 |     // Each JavaScript file is rooted in a script node, so we'll only
59 |     // have line number information inside the script node.

===== M0232 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 187-204
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
187 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
188 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
189 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
190 |     if (parent == null
191 |         || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION
192 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
193 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
194 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
195 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
196 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
197 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
198 |       }
199 | 
200 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
201 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |   }

===== M0241 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 205-229
Documentation:

Method code:
205 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
206 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
207 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
208 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
209 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
213 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
214 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
215 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
216 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
217 |     } else {
218 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
219 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
220 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
221 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
222 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
223 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
224 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
225 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
226 |       }
227 |     }
228 |     return irNode;
229 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 467-481
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |       } else {
477 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 |     return unknown;
481 |   }

Method code:
1667 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1668 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1669 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1670 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1671 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.

===== M0298 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.DuplicateDeclarationHandler.onRedeclaration(Scope,String,Node,CompilerInput)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 648-702
Documentation:
/**
     * Remove duplicate VAR declarations encountered discovered during
     * scope creation.
     */
Method code:
648 |     @Override
649 |     public void onRedeclaration(
650 |         Scope s, String name, Node n, CompilerInput input) {
651 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.NAME);
652 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
653 |       Var v = s.getVar(name);
654 | 
655 |       if (v != null && s.isGlobal()) {
656 |         // We allow variables to be duplicate declared if one
657 |         // declaration appears in source and the other in externs.
658 |         // This deals with issues where a browser built-in is declared
659 |         // in one browser but not in another.
660 |         if (v.isExtern() && !input.isExtern()) {
661 |           if (hasOkDuplicateDeclaration.add(v)) {
662 |             return;
663 |           }
664 |         }
665 |       }
666 | 
667 |       // If name is "arguments", Var maybe null.
668 |       if (v != null && v.getParentNode().getType() == Token.CATCH) {
669 |         // Redeclaration of a catch expression variable is hard to model
670 |         // without support for "with" expressions.
671 |         // The EcmaScript spec (section 12.14), declares that a catch
672 |         // "catch (e) {}" is handled like "with ({'e': e}) {}" so that
673 |         // "var e" would refer to the scope variable, but any following
674 |         // reference would still refer to "e" of the catch expression.
675 |         // Until we have support for this disallow it.
676 |         // Currently the Scope object adds the catch expression to the
677 |         // function scope, which is technically not true but a good
678 |         // approximation for most uses.
679 | 
680 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Consider improving how scope handles catch
681 |         // expression.
682 | 
683 |         // Use the name of the var before it was made unique.
684 |         name = MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.ContextualRenameInverter.getOrginalName(
685 |             name);
686 |         compiler.report(
687 |             JSError.make(
688 |                 input.getName(), n,
689 |                 CATCH_BLOCK_VAR_ERROR, name));
690 |       } else if (v != null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
691 |         if (v.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR) {
692 |           s.undeclare(v);
693 |           s.declare(name, n, n.getJSType(), v.input);
694 |           replaceVarWithAssignment(v.getNameNode(), v.getParentNode(),
695 |               v.getParentNode().getParent());
696 |         }
697 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
698 |         Preconditions.checkState(parent.hasOneChild());
699 | 
700 |         replaceVarWithAssignment(n, parent, parent.getParent());
701 |       }
702 |     }

===== M0301 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

===== M0313 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 82-112
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
83 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
84 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
85 | 
86 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
87 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
88 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
89 | 
90 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
91 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
92 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
93 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
94 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Args: Declare function variables
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
99 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
100 |            a = a.getNext()) {
101 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
102 |         declareVar(a);
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // Body
106 |       scanVars(body, n);
107 |     } else {
108 |       // It's the global block
109 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
110 |       scanVars(n, null);
111 |     }
112 |   }

317 |     @Override
318 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
319 |       switch (n.getType()) {

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

===== M0338 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1595-1632
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1595 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1596 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1597 |       try {
1598 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1599 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1600 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1601 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1602 |             return false;
1603 |           }
1604 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1607 |           return false;
1608 |         } else {
1609 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1610 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1611 |               return false;
1612 |             }
1613 |           }
1614 |         }
1615 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1616 |         return false;
1617 |       }
1618 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1619 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1620 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1621 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1622 |         return false;
1623 |       }
1624 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1625 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1626 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1627 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     }
1631 |     return true;
1632 |   }

583 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 118-282
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Is this node a function declaration? A function declaration is a function
   * that has a name that is added to the current scope (i.e. a function that
   * is not part of a expression; see {@link #isFunctionExpression}).
   */
Method code:
1870 |   static boolean isFunctionDeclaration(Node n) {
1871 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && isStatement(n);
1872 |   }

===== M0350 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 260-282
Documentation:

Method code:
260 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
261 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
262 |       return;
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     try {
266 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
267 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
268 | 
269 |       sourceName = "";
270 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
271 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
272 | 
273 |       for (Node root : roots) {
274 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
275 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       popScope();
279 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
280 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
281 |     }
282 |   }

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 141-167
Documentation:

Method code:
141 |   @Override
142 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     switch (n.getType()) {
144 |       case Token.NAME:
145 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
146 |         if (newName != null) {
147 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
148 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
149 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
150 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
151 |           }
152 |           n.setString(newName);
153 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
154 |         }
155 |         break;
156 | 
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
159 |         nameStack.pop();
160 |         break;
161 | 
162 |       case Token.CATCH:
163 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
164 |         nameStack.pop();
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 |   }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 118-149
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   @Override
119 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
120 |     new NodeTraversal(
121 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
122 |         .traverse(root);
123 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
124 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
125 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
126 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
127 |     }
128 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
129 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
130 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
131 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
132 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
133 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
134 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
135 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
136 |     //   function f() {
137 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
138 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
139 |     //   }
140 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
141 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
142 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
143 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
144 |         .process(externs, root);
145 | 
146 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
147 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
148 |     }
149 |   }

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 496-542
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
496 |     private void extractForInitializer(
497 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
498 | 
499 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
500 |         next = c.getNext();
501 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
502 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
503 |         switch (c.getType()) {
504 |           case Token.LABEL:
505 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
506 |             break;
507 |           case Token.FOR:
508 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
509 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
510 |               if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
511 |                 // Transform:
512 |                 //    for (var a in b) {}
513 |                 // to:
514 |                 //    var a; for (a in b) {};
515 |                 Node newStatement = first.cloneTree();
516 |                 Node name = first.removeFirstChild();
517 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
518 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
519 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
520 |               }
521 |             } else if (c.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
522 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
523 |               Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
524 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
525 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
526 | 
527 |               Node newStatement;
528 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
529 |               // but are handled differently.
530 |               if (init.getType() == Token.VAR) {
531 |                 newStatement = init;
532 |               } else {
533 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
534 |               }
535 | 
536 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
537 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
538 |             }
539 |             break;
540 |         }
541 |       }
542 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)

Original range: 602-614
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
602 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
603 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
604 |     if (typeSet == null) {
605 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
606 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
607 |     }
608 | 
609 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
610 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
611 | 
612 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
613 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
614 |   }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.DefaultRedeclarationHandler.onRedeclaration(Scope,String,Node,CompilerInput)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 184-222
Documentation:

Method code:
184 |     public void onRedeclaration(
185 |         Scope s, String name, Node n, CompilerInput input) {
186 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
187 | 
188 |       // Don't allow multiple variables to be declared at the top level scope
189 |       if (scope.isGlobal()) {
190 |         Scope.Var origVar = scope.getVar(name);
191 |         Node origParent = origVar.getParentNode();
192 |         if (origParent.getType() == Token.CATCH &&
193 |             parent.getType() == Token.CATCH) {
194 |           // Okay, both are 'catch(x)' variables.
195 |           return;
196 |         }
197 | 
198 |         boolean allowDupe = false;
199 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
200 |         if (info == null) {
201 |           info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
202 |         }
203 |         allowDupe =
204 |             info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
205 | 
206 |         if (!allowDupe) {
207 |           compiler.report(
208 |               JSError.make(sourceName, n,
209 |                            VAR_MULTIPLY_DECLARED_ERROR,
210 |                            name,
211 |                            (origVar.input != null
212 |                             ? origVar.input.getName()
213 |                             : "??")));
214 |         }
215 |       } else if (name.equals(ARGUMENTS) && !NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
216 |         // Disallow shadowing "arguments" as we can't handle with our current
217 |         // scope modeling.
218 |         compiler.report(
219 |             JSError.make(sourceName, n,
220 |                 VAR_ARGUMENTS_SHADOWED_ERROR));
221 |       }
222 |     }

396 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
397 |       List<JSModule> modules) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

===== M0386 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 117-170
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.VAR:
120 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
121 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
122 |              child != null;) {
123 |           Node next = child.getNext();
124 |           declareVar(child);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any

===== M0411 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0415 =====

/**
     * Do normalizations that introduce new siblings or parents.
     */
