434 |     private void doStatementNormalizations(
435 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
436 |       if (n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
437 |         normalizeLabels(n);
438 |       }
439 | 
440 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs and LABELs, as all these
441 |       // are the only legal place for VARs and FOR statements.
442 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n) || n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
443 |         extractForInitializer(n, null, null);
444 |       }
445 | 
446 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs, as all these
447 |       // are the only legal place for VARs.
448 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
449 |         splitVarDeclarations(n);
450 |       }
451 | 
452 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
453 |         moveNamedFunctions(n.getLastChild());
454 |       }
455 |     }

===== M0419 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 512-569
Documentation:

Method code:
512 |     @Override
513 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
514 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
515 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
516 |       if (name == null) {
517 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
518 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
519 |           errorReporter.error(
520 |             "unnamed function statement",
521 |             sourceName,
522 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
523 |         }
524 |         name = new Name();
525 |         name.setIdentifier("");
526 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
527 |       }
528 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
529 |       Node newName = transform(name);
530 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
531 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
532 |         // declaration.
533 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
534 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
535 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
536 |         // same line as the function keyword.
537 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
538 |             functionNode.getLp();
539 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
540 |       }
541 | 
542 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
543 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
544 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
545 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
546 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
547 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
548 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
549 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
550 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
551 |       if (fnName != null) {
552 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
553 |       } else {
554 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
555 |       }
556 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
557 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
558 | 
559 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
560 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
561 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
562 |       }
563 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
564 | 
565 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
566 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
567 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);

===== M0436 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 286-310
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
287 |     if (prototype == null) {
288 |       return false;
289 |     }
290 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
291 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
292 |       return false;
293 |     }
294 | 
295 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
296 |     this.prototype = prototype;
297 | 
298 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
299 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
300 |       if (superClass != null) {
301 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
302 |       }
303 |     }
304 | 
305 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
306 |       clearCachedValues();
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     return true;
310 |   }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 632-658
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
632 |   @Override
633 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
634 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
635 |       return false;
636 |     }
637 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
638 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
639 |       return false;
640 |     }
641 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
642 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
643 |         return this == that;
644 |       }
645 |       return false;
646 |     }
647 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
648 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
649 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
650 |       }
651 |       return false;
652 |     }
653 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
654 |       return false;
655 |     }
656 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
657 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
658 |   }

105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.

123 |         }
124 |         break;

129 |

131 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);

/**
   * Determines whether a node represents an object literal key
   * (e.g. key1 in {key1: value1, key2: value2}).
   *
   * @param node A node
   * @param parent The node's parent
   */
Method code:
2021 |   static boolean isObjectLitKey(Node node, Node parent) {
2022 |     switch (node.getType()) {
2023 |       case Token.NUMBER:
2024 |       case Token.STRING:
2025 |         return parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT;

===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.annotateConstantsByConvention(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 350-379
Documentation:
/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
350 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
351 |       Preconditions.checkState(
352 |           n.getType() == Token.NAME
353 |           || n.getType() == Token.STRING
354 |           || n.getType() == Token.GET
355 |           || n.getType() == Token.SET);
356 | 
357 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
358 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
359 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
360 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
361 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
362 |           (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
363 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
364 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME || isProperty) {
365 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
366 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
367 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
368 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
369 |           if (assertOnChange) {
370 |             String name = n.getString();
371 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
372 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
373 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
374 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
375 |           }
376 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
377 |         }
378 |       }
379 |     }
